/// The innermost section holding a line: its number (0 for the opening) and heading.
fn section_of(found: &[(usize, String, usize, usize)], line: usize) -> (usize, &str) {
    found.iter().enumerate().filter(|(_, s)| s.2 <= line && line < s.3).max_by_key(|(_, s)| s.0)
        .map(|(n, s)| (n + 1, s.1.as_str())).unwrap_or((0, "Opening"))
}

/// The alternatives of a search: `a|b` and `a OR b`.
fn alternatives(words: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    words.split('|').flat_map(|part| part.split(" OR ")).map(|alternative| alternative.trim().to_owned()).filter(|alternative| !alternative.is_empty()).collect()
}

/// A word as written, and its singular (`loops` finds "loop", `queries` "query").
fn forms(word: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut forms = vec![word.to_owned()];
    if let Some(stem) = word.strip_suffix("ies").filter(|stem| stem.len() > 2) { forms.push(format!("{stem}y")); }
    else if let Some(stem) = word.strip_suffix('s').filter(|stem| stem.len() > 2 && !stem.ends_with('s')) { forms.push(stem.to_owned()); }
    forms
}

// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.1 — `--find a b` is every word on one line; `a|b` and `a OR b` are either.
/// Line numbers (0-based) whose text holds an alternative: its phrase, or
/// every one of its words (each as written or singular).
fn mentions(card: &str, words: &str) -> Vec<usize> {
    let lines: Vec<String> = card.lines().map(str::to_lowercase).collect();
    let mut found = Vec::new();
    for alternative in alternatives(words) {
        let phrase = alternative.to_lowercase();
        let each: Vec<Vec<String>> = words_of(&phrase).split_whitespace().map(forms).collect();
        found.extend((0..lines.len()).filter(|&i| {
            if lines[i].contains(&phrase) { return true; }
            let line = words_of(&lines[i]);
            !each.is_empty() && each.iter().all(|forms| forms.iter().any(|w| line.contains(w.as_str())))
        }));
    }
    found.sort_unstable();
    found.dedup();
    found
}

/// Lines holding any one of several words (connectives dropped), for a
/// search whose words no single line holds together.
fn any_mentions(card: &str, words: &str) -> Vec<usize> {
    if alternatives(words).len() != 1 { return Vec::new(); }
    let each: Vec<&str> = words.split_whitespace().filter(|w| !["or", "and", "OR", "AND"].contains(w)).collect();
    if each.len() < 2 { return Vec::new(); }
    mentions(card, &each.join("|"))
}

/// `guide <card> --section <words>`: the title line and one section, found by
/// its number in the index (`0` or `opening` for the text before the first
/// heading), its heading (any case, punctuation ignored), a unique prefix, a
/// unique substring, or headings holding every word. When no heading matches
/// but the words occur, the section holding most of their mentions prints, or
/// every mention when they spread; only words no line mentions refuse, naming
/// the nearest heading by spelling.
pub fn section(name: &str, card: &str, words: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
    let found = sections(card);
    let lines: Vec<&str> = card.lines().collect();
    let title = lines.first().filter(|line| line.starts_with("# ")).map(|line| format!("{line}\n\n")).unwrap_or_default();
    let wanted = words_of(words);
    let print = |start: usize, end: usize| format!("{title}{}", lines[start..end].join("\n").trim_end());
    let opening = opening_end(card, &found);
    if matches!(wanted.as_str(), "0" | "opening" | "intro" | "introduction") {
        return Ok(lines[..opening].join("\n").trim_end().to_owned());
    }
    if let Ok(n) = wanted.parse::<usize>() {
        return match found.get(n.wrapping_sub(1)) {
            Some((_, _, start, end)) => Ok(print(*start, *end)),
            // `1000` is an alias of the bounds card, not its thousandth section.
            None => alias_inside(name, card, words).ok_or_else(|| format!("guide {name} has no section {n}\n{}", section_index(name, card))),
        };
    }
    // An alias that names a section of this card lands there (`subpaths` would match two headings).
    if super::alias_section(words).is_some_and(|(target, _)| target == name) {
        if let Some(landing) = alias_inside(name, card, words) { return Ok(landing); }
    }
    let numbered: Vec<(usize, &(usize, String, usize, usize), String)> = found.iter().enumerate().map(|(n, s)| (n + 1, s, words_of(&s.1))).collect();
    let stages: [&dyn Fn(&str) -> bool; 4] = [
        &|heading| heading == wanted,
        &|heading| heading.starts_with(&wanted),
        &|heading| heading.contains(&wanted),
        &|heading| wanted.split(' ').all(|w| heading.split(' ').any(|h| h.starts_with(w))),
    ];
    for stage in stages {
        if wanted.is_empty() { break; }
        let matches: Vec<_> = numbered.iter().filter(|(_, _, heading)| stage(heading)).collect();
        match matches.as_slice() {
            [] => continue,
            [(_, (_, _, start, end), _)] => return Ok(print(*start, *end)),
//! SQLite erases booleans to integers. Recover direct column types from the
//! adopted schema and SQL projection, never by guessing from names or 0/1.
// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.26 — positional JSON stays compatible; boolean recovery is presentation on the operator's adopted snapshot.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#10-wave-25-day-two-friction-dx8 — direct schema-proven media cells use their ordinary wire JSON in object rows.
use snapback4_core::{inspection::InspectionColumnKind as Kind, schema::{ColumnType, Schema}};
use sqlparser::{ast::{Expr, ObjectName, Query, SelectItem, SelectItemQualifiedWildcardKind, SetExpr, Statement, TableFactor}, dialect::SQLiteDialect, parser::Parser};
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};

type Columns = Vec<(String, Kind)>;
type Tables = BTreeMap<String, Columns>;

pub(crate) fn kinds(sql: &str, schema: &Schema, count: usize, retained: bool) -> Result<Vec<Kind>, String> {
    let mut tables: Tables = schema.visible_tables().map(|(name, table)| {
        let mut columns = vec![("id".into(), Kind::Plain)];
        columns.extend(table.columns.iter().filter(|(name, _)| *name != "id")
            .map(|(name, kind)| (name.to_ascii_lowercase(), if matches!(kind.required(), ColumnType::Bool) { Kind::Bool } else if kind.contains_asset() { Kind::Media } else { Kind::Plain })));
        if retained {columns.extend([("_snapback_state".into(),Kind::Plain),("_snapback_source".into(),Kind::Plain),("_snapback_columns".into(),Kind::Plain)]);}
        (name.to_ascii_lowercase(), columns)
    }).collect();
    let statements = Parser::parse_sql(&SQLiteDialect {}, sql).map_err(|e| format!("--json-objects SQL projection: {e}; use --json for SQLite-specific syntax"))?;
    let [Statement::Query(query)] = statements.as_slice() else { return Err("--json-objects takes a SELECT".into()); };
    let columns = query_columns(query, &mut tables)?;
    if columns.len() != count { return Err("--json-objects cannot determine this projection's column types; select explicit columns or use --json".into()); }
    Ok(columns.into_iter().map(|(_, kind)| kind).collect())
}

fn name(name: &ObjectName) -> Option<String> {
    // SQLite schema qualification bypasses CTE shadowing; do not erase it.
    if name.0.len() != 1 { return None; }
    name.0.last()?.as_ident().map(|id| id.value.to_ascii_lowercase())
}

fn query_columns(query: &Query, inherited: &mut Tables) -> Result<Columns, String> {
    let mut tables = inherited.clone();
    if let Some(with) = &query.with {
        if with.recursive { return Err("--json-objects does not infer recursive CTE types; use --json".into()); }
        for cte in &with.cte_tables {
            if tables.contains_key(&cte.alias.name.value.to_ascii_lowercase()) {
                return Err("--json-objects does not infer shadowing CTEs; use --json".into());
            }
            let mut columns = query_columns(&cte.query, &mut tables)?;
            for (column, alias) in columns.iter_mut().zip(&cte.alias.columns) { column.0 = alias.name.value.to_ascii_lowercase(); }
            tables.insert(cte.alias.name.value.to_ascii_lowercase(), columns);
        }
    }
    set_columns(&query.body, &mut tables)
}

fn relation(factor: &TableFactor, tables: &mut Tables) -> Result<(String, Columns), String> {
    match factor {
        TableFactor::Table { name: object, alias, args: None, .. } => {
            let key = name(object).ok_or("--json-objects does not infer schema-qualified relations; use --json")?;
            let columns = tables.get(&key).ok_or("--json-objects needs application tables or typed CTEs")?.clone();
            Ok((alias.as_ref().map(|a| a.name.value.to_ascii_lowercase()).unwrap_or(key), columns))
        }
        TableFactor::Derived { subquery, alias, .. } => Ok((alias.as_ref().map(|a| a.name.value.to_ascii_lowercase()).unwrap_or_default(), query_columns(subquery, tables)?)),
        _ => Err("--json-objects cannot infer this table expression; use --json".into()),
    }
}

fn set_columns(body: &SetExpr, tables: &mut Tables) -> Result<Columns, String> {
    match body {
        SetExpr::Query(q) => query_columns(q, tables),
        SetExpr::SetOperation { left, right, .. } => {
            let mut left = set_columns(left, tables)?;
            let right = set_columns(right, tables)?;
            if left.len() != right.len() { return Err("incompatible SQL projections".into()); }
            for (l, r) in left.iter_mut().zip(right) { if l.1 != r.1 { l.1 = Kind::Plain; } }
            Ok(left)
        }
        SetExpr::Select(select) => {
            let mut sources = Vec::new();
            for from in &select.from {
                sources.push(relation(&from.relation, tables)?);
                for join in &from.joins { sources.push(relation(&join.relation, tables)?); }
            }
            let mut output = Vec::new();
            for item in &select.projection {
                match item {
                    SelectItem::Wildcard(_) => output.extend(sources.iter().flat_map(|(_, cols)| cols.clone())),
                    SelectItem::QualifiedWildcard(SelectItemQualifiedWildcardKind::ObjectName(object), _) => {
                        let key = name(object).ok_or("unsupported wildcard")?;
                        let (_, cols) = sources.iter().find(|(alias, _)| *alias == key).ok_or("unknown wildcard table")?;
                        output.extend(cols.clone());
                    }
                    SelectItem::UnnamedExpr(expr) => {
                        let mut direct = expr;
                        while let Expr::Nested(inner) = direct { direct = inner; }
                        let label = match direct {
                            Expr::Identifier(id) => id.value.clone(),
                            Expr::CompoundIdentifier(ids) => ids.last().unwrap().value.clone(),
                            _ => expr.to_string(),
                        };
                        output.push((label.to_ascii_lowercase(), kind(expr, &sources)));
                    }
                    SelectItem::ExprWithAlias { expr, alias } => output.push((alias.value.to_ascii_lowercase(), kind(expr, &sources))),
                    _ => return Err("unsupported object-row projection; use --json".into()),
                }
            }
            let mut names = BTreeSet::new();
            if output.iter().any(|(name, _)| !names.insert(name)) {
                return Err("--json-objects needs unique names in every projection; alias duplicate columns".into());
            }
            Ok(output)
        }
        _ => Err("--json-objects needs a SELECT projection; use --json".into()),
    }
}

fn kind(expr: &Expr, sources: &[(String, Columns)]) -> Kind {
    let (table, column) = match expr {
        Expr::Nested(expr) => return kind(expr, sources),
        Expr::Identifier(id) => (None, &id.value),
        Expr::CompoundIdentifier(ids) if ids.len() == 2 => (Some(ids[0].value.to_ascii_lowercase()), &ids[1].value),
        _ => return Kind::Plain, // Computed SQL expressions retain SQLite's own types.
    };
    let found: Vec<Kind> = sources.iter().filter(|(alias, _)| table.as_ref().is_none_or(|t| t == alias))
        .flat_map(|(_, cols)| cols.iter().filter(|(name, _)| name.eq_ignore_ascii_case(column)).map(|(_, kind)| *kind)).collect();
    // An unqualified duplicate only reaches execution when SQLite resolved it
    // through USING/NATURAL. All contributors must agree before typing it bool.
    found.first().copied().filter(|first| found.iter().all(|kind| kind == first)).unwrap_or(Kind::Plain)
}

import ts from 'typescript';
import { getEncoding } from 'js-tiktoken';
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync, mkdirSync } from 'node:fs';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
const root = new URL('../', import.meta.url), dist = new URL('dist/', root);
const out = new URL('../../crates/snapback4-lang/src/guide/', root);
const encoding = getEncoding('o200k_base');
const modules = {

  client: ['createClient','ClientOptions','Client','Observed','ObserveOptions','Read','Write','Signal','Cursor','AssetRead','Uploaded','settleMutation','Session','SignIn','Query','Mutation','MutateOptions','mintId','idFrom','Ready','SettledRead','Refusal','MutationPromise','UploadOptions'],
  auth: ['signup','login','SignIn','Session','SignInOptions','guest','SessionStore','memorySessionStore','restoreSession','keepSession','forgetSession','logout','logoutAll','refreshSession','narrowSession'],
};
function digest(module, names) {
  const file = fileURLToPath(new URL(`${module}.d.ts`, dist));
  const program = ts.createProgram([file], { noResolve: false, skipLibCheck: true, target: ts.ScriptTarget.ESNext, module: ts.ModuleKind.NodeNext, moduleResolution: ts.ModuleResolutionKind.NodeNext });
  const checker = program.getTypeChecker(), source = program.getSourceFile(file);
  const exports = checker.getExportsOfModule(checker.getSymbolAtLocation(source));
  const hashes = new Map();
  function declarations(name, compact = true) {
    let symbol = exports.find(e => e.name === name);
    if (!symbol) {
      const dependency = ts.createSourceFile('contract.d.ts', readFileSync(new URL('contract.d.ts',dist),'utf8'), ts.ScriptTarget.Latest, true);
      const node = dependency.statements.find(s => s.name?.text === name);
      if (!node) throw Error(`guide api ${module}: type ${name} missing`);
      hashes.set('contract.d.ts', createHash('sha256').update(dependency.text).digest('hex'));
      return `// ${name}: import type from snapback4/contract\n` + node.getText(dependency).replace(/\/\*\*[\s\S]*?\*\//g,'').replace(/\s+/g,' ');
    }
    if (symbol.flags & ts.SymbolFlags.Alias) symbol = checker.getAliasedSymbol(symbol);
    return (symbol.declarations ?? []).map(node => {
      const source = node.getSourceFile();
      hashes.set(source.fileName.split('/').at(-1), createHash('sha256').update(source.text).digest('hex'));
      let text = (ts.isVariableDeclaration(node) ? 'export const ' : '') + node.getText(source);
      // Client's core methods replace the much larger advanced-lifecycle surface.
      if (compact && name === 'Client' && ts.isInterfaceDeclaration(node)) {
        const wanted = ['query','mutate','observe','upload','asset','close','watch'];
        const members = node.members.filter(n => wanted.includes(n.name?.getText(source)));
        text = `export interface Client { ${members.map(n => n.getText(source)).join(' ')} /* other methods: dist/contract.d.ts */ }`;
      }
      return text.replace(/\/\*\*[\s\S]*?\*\//g, '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').replace(/^export declare /, 'export ');
    }).join('\n');
  }
  const pieces = names.map(name => ({ name, text: declarations(name) }));
  let title = `# API ${module}: exact signature digest\n\nImport from "snapback4/${module}". Generated from dist declarations; types named below retain their declaration meaning.\n`;
  if (module === 'client') title += 'observe returns getSnapshot/subscribe/close; query returns Read, mutate returns Write; asset returns a URL handle or denied. Upload each image column separately. sentResult is from snapback4/react or contract.\n';
  if (module === 'proof') title += 'Production: startServe(dir,{provisionUsers:{roles:{member:2,admin:2},provision:api.provision}}) returns host.sessions, shaped {member:[account,account],admin:[account,account]}. Account = {kind,role,email,password,principal,token,expiresAt}. Signup grants no role. The private persistent roster is DEPLOY/proof-accounts/default.json (0600): {version:1,plan,entries:[{role,index,email,password,id,done,principal?,args?}]}; keep it with its store. provisionAccounts reuses this roster; freshUser creates an isolated proof identity instead. Read guide production --section "Serve script and reusable accounts" for partial-setup recovery; never read proof.js.\n';
  if (module === 'proof') title += 'Use probe:check for verification; probe:serve stays up until SIGTERM (deliberate harness run: --hold).\n';
  const render = entries => `${title}\n\`\`\`ts\n${entries.map(p => p.text).join('\n')}\n\`\`\`\n\nOther exports/options: dist/${module}.d.ts; follow its named imports only on demand.\n`;
  const selected = [];
  for (const piece of pieces) { if (encoding.encode(render([...selected,piece])).length <= 2800) selected.push(piece); }
  const omitted = pieces.filter(p => !selected.includes(p)).map(p => p.name);
  let text = render(selected);
  if (omitted.length) text += `Additional requested declarations in dist/${module}.d.ts: ${omitted.join(', ')}.\n`;
  // A source digest covers selected and omitted requested declarations, so drift fails.
  text += `\nDeclaration fingerprint: ${createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify([...hashes].sort())).digest('hex')}\n`;
  if (encoding.encode(text).length > 3000) throw Error(`guide api ${module} exceeds 3000 tokens`);
  if (module !== 'proof') {
    const complete = `${title}\n\`\`\`ts\n${exports.map(s => declarations(s.name, false)).join('\n')}\n\`\`\`\n`;
    for (const target of [new URL(`api_${module}_full.md`,out), new URL(`guide-api/${module}-full.md`,dist)]) {
      if (process.argv.includes('--check')) { if (readFileSync(target,'utf8') !== complete) throw Error(`guide api ${module} --full drift`); }
      else writeFileSync(target, complete);
    }
  }
  return text;
}
for (const [module,names] of Object.entries(modules)) {
  const text = digest(module,names), tracked = new URL(`api_${module}.md`,out), packed = new URL(`guide-api/${module}.md`,dist);
  mkdirSync(new URL('guide-api/',dist), { recursive:true });
  if (process.argv.includes('--check')) { if (readFileSync(tracked,'utf8') !== text || readFileSync(packed,'utf8') !== text) throw Error(`guide api ${module} drift; rebuild client`); }
  else { writeFileSync(tracked,text); writeFileSync(packed,text); }
  console.log(`guide api ${module}: ${encoding.encode(text).length}/3000 tokens`);
}
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#3 — generated proofs use the published kit and runtime ports.

use serde_json::{Value, json};
use super::{Options, pretty};

const README_PROOFS: &str = "\n## Frontend proofs\n\n`npm run frontend:proof` builds its own dist, then proves the starter in Chromium.\nAfter replacing the screen, add your business assertions to scripts/frontend-proof.mjs.\nNew starters include `npm run day2:proof`, saved versions and id-based conservation\nacross two rollback/forward cycles. Add authenticated business assertions at\nBUSINESS_ASSERTION_BEGIN; return deliberately v1-written table/id and changed-field lists there.\nMark deliberate deletes with deleted:true; the proof checks they stay absent.\nExisting apps can add it with `snapback4 init --adapt --day2 --apply`.\nSet PORT_LO and PORT_HI at run time to confine proof servers and launchers.\nSet CHROME=/path/to/chromium or install Playwright's Chromium.\n";
const PREVIOUS_40_README_PROOFS: &str = "\n## Frontend proofs\n\n`npm run frontend:proof` builds its own dist, then proves the starter in Chromium.\nAfter replacing the screen, add your business assertions to scripts/frontend-proof.mjs.\nDay-two scaffolding is opt-in: `snapback4 init --adapt --day2 --apply` captures\ncurrent sources and fixtures in versions/v1 and versions/v2 and adds day2:proof.\nReplace its BUSINESS_ASSERTION_BEGIN block before running `npm run day2:proof`.\nSet PORT_LO and PORT_HI at run time to confine proof servers and launchers.\nSet CHROME=/path/to/chromium or install Playwright's Chromium.\n";
const PREVIOUS_30_README_PROOFS: &str = "\n## Frontend and day-two proofs\n\nRun\n`npm run frontend:proof` (it builds its own dist) to prove sign-in and the starter form in Chromium.\nAfter replacing the screen, add your business assertions to scripts/frontend-proof.mjs.\n`npm run day2:proof` proves saved-source deploy,\nrollback and compatible forward using versions/v1 and versions/v2. Replace the marked business assertion before it can pass.\nSet `PORT_LO` and `PORT_HI` at run time to confine proof servers and launchers;\nunset means allocated proof ports and the interactive starter defaults.\nSet `CHROME=/path/to/chromium` or install Playwright's Chromium (`npx playwright-core install chromium`).\n";
const PREVIOUS_README_PROOFS: &str = "\n## Frontend and day-two proofs\n\nAfter `npm run build` (web) or `npm run build:web` (Expo), run\n`npm run frontend:proof` to prove sign-in and the starter form in Chromium.\nAfter replacing the screen, add your business assertions to scripts/frontend-proof.mjs.\n`npm run day2:proof -- versions/v1.q versions/v2.q` proves saved-source deploy,\nrollback and compatible forward without replacing your working schema.\nSet `PORT_LO` and `PORT_HI` at run time to confine proof servers and launchers;\nunset means allocated proof ports and the interactive starter defaults.\nSet `CHROME=/path/to/chromium` or install Playwright's Chromium (`npx playwright-core install chromium`).\n";
/// Only the exact generated appendix is ignored for historical byte recognition.
pub(super) fn without_readme(text: &str) -> &str { text.strip_suffix(README_PROOFS).or_else(|| text.strip_suffix(PREVIOUS_40_README_PROOFS)).or_else(|| text.strip_suffix(PREVIOUS_30_README_PROOFS)).or_else(|| text.strip_suffix(PREVIOUS_README_PROOFS)).unwrap_or(text) }
pub(super) fn readme(mut text: String) -> String {
    if !text.contains("`npm run frontend:proof`") { text.push_str(README_PROOFS); }
    text
}

pub(super) fn files(files: &mut Vec<(&'static str, String)>, options: &Options, backend: u16, frontend: u16, web: bool, shape: &super::starter::Shape) {
    let has_frontend = true;
    let has_write_scenario = shape.body == super::starter::Body::Todos || options.flags.contains_key("template");
    for (_, text) in files.iter_mut().filter(|(name, _)| *name == "README.md") {
        if has_frontend { *text = readme(std::mem::take(text)); }
    }
    for (name, text) in files.iter_mut() {
        if *name == "vite.config.mts" { *text = runtime_vite(text, backend, frontend); }
    }
    let proof = if has_write_scenario { frontend_proof(include_str!("frontend-proof.mjs"), options.flags.get("template").map(String::as_str), web) } else { frontend_scaffold(web) };
    let dir = options.positionals.first().map(std::path::Path::new).unwrap_or_else(|| std::path::Path::new("."));
    let sources = if let Ok(Some(own)) = super::own_schema(dir) {
        own.files.iter().filter_map(|name| std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("snapback").join(name)).ok().map(|text| (name.clone(), text))).collect::<Vec<_>>()
    } else { files.iter().find(|(name, _)| *name == "snapback/schema.q").map(|(_, text)| vec![("schema.q".to_owned(), text.clone())]).unwrap_or_default() };
    let day2 = options.flags.contains_key("day2") || !dir.join("package.json").is_file();
    if day2 {
        // Adapt's fallible preflight reports invalid captures before applying files.
        if let Ok(frozen) = super::frozen::files(dir, &sources) { files.extend(frozen); }
    }
    for version in ["v1", "v2"].into_iter().filter(|_| day2) {
        if dir.join("versions").join(version).exists() { continue; }
        // Init is a one-shot CLI operation. Destinations live for this invocation's
        // plan, whose existing interface uses static generated names.
        for (name, text) in &sources {
            let path: &'static str = Box::leak(format!("versions/{version}/{name}").into_boxed_str());
            files.push((path, text.clone()));
        }
        let marker: &'static str = Box::leak(format!("versions/{version}/fixtures/.gitkeep").into_boxed_str());
        files.push((marker, String::new()));
        let mut fixtures = files.iter().filter_map(|(name, text)| name.strip_prefix("fixtures/").map(|name| (name.to_owned(), text.clone()))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if fixtures.is_empty() {
            if let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(dir.join("fixtures")) {
                for entry in entries.flatten().filter(|entry| entry.path().extension().is_some_and(|ext| ext == "jsonl") || entry.file_name() == "test.sweep.json") {
                    if let Ok(text) = std::fs::read_to_string(entry.path()) { fixtures.push((entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().into_owned(), text)); }
                }
            }
        }
        for (name, text) in fixtures {
            let path: &'static str = Box::leak(format!("versions/{version}/fixtures/{name}").into_boxed_str());
            files.push((path, text));
        }
    }
    if has_frontend {
        if shape.password {
            let proof=if options.flags.contains_key("password") && super::own_schema(dir).ok().flatten().is_none() {
                frontend_proof(include_str!("frontend-proof.mjs"),None,web).replace("BUILD_SCRIPT",if web {"build"}else{"build:web"}).replace("Task","Note").replace("todo-results","note-results").replace("RESULTS_SCOPE",if web {"page.getByRole(\"list\")"}else{"page.getByTestId(\"todo-results\")"})
            } else {proof};
            files.extend(super::password_proof::files(&sources,proof));
        }
        else {files.push(("scripts/frontend-proof.mjs",proof));}
    }
    if day2 { files.push(("scripts/day2-proof.mjs", include_str!("day2-proof.mjs").to_owned())); }
    files.push(("scripts/launch.mjs", include_str!("launch.mjs").replace("BACKEND", &backend.to_string()).replace("FRONTEND", &frontend.to_string())));
    for (name, text) in files.iter_mut().filter(|(name, _)| *name == "package.json") {
        let mut package: Value = serde_json::from_str(text).unwrap();
        if has_frontend { package["scripts"]["frontend:proof"] = json!("node scripts/frontend-proof.mjs"); }
        if shape.password {package["scripts"]["probe:serve"]=json!("node scripts/probe-serve.mjs");package["scripts"]["probe:check"]=json!("node scripts/probe-check.mjs");}
        if day2 { package["scripts"]["day2:proof"] = json!("node scripts/day2-proof.mjs versions/v1 versions/v2"); }
        if (package["snapback4"].is_null() || package["snapback4"].is_object()) && (package["snapback4"]["starter"].is_null() || package["snapback4"]["starter"].is_object()) {
            package["snapback4"]["starter"]["day2"] = json!(day2);
        }
        if has_frontend {
            package["scripts"]["proof:web"] = json!("node scripts/frontend-proof.mjs");
            package["devDependencies"]["playwright-core"] = json!("1.55.1");
        }
        package["scripts"]["backend"] = json!("node scripts/launch.mjs backend");
        if web {
            package["scripts"]["dev"] = json!("node scripts/launch.mjs vite");
            package["scripts"]["preview"] = json!("node scripts/launch.mjs vite-preview");
        } else {
            package["scripts"]["start"] = json!("node scripts/launch.mjs expo");
            package["scripts"]["web"] = json!("node scripts/launch.mjs expo-web");
            package["scripts"]["native:smoke"] = json!("node scripts/launch.mjs native-smoke");
        }
        *text = pretty(&package);
        let _ = name;
    }
}

pub(super) fn frontend_proof(source: &str, template: Option<&str>, web: bool) -> String {
    // Social keeps account controls on its Profile tab; prove the actual route.
    let mut proof = source.replace("// TEMPLATE_READY", if template == Some("social") {
        "await page.getByText(\"Profile\", { exact: true }).click();"
    } else { "" });
    proof = proof.replace("// SHARED_IMPORT", if template.is_none() { "import { matchesFilter } from \"../src/shared.ts\";\nassert.equal(matchesFilter(false, \"open\"), true);" } else { "" });
    proof = proof.replace("// LABELLED_CONTROLS", if template.is_none() && web { "const filter = page.getByRole(\"combobox\", { name: \"Task status\", exact: true });\n    await filter.selectOption(\"open\");\n    await filter.selectOption(\"all\");" } else if template.is_none() { "await page.getByLabel(\"Write status\", { exact: true }).waitFor();" } else { "" });
    if template == Some("social") {
        proof = proof.replace("getByLabel(\"Task\"", "getByLabel(\"Display name\"")
            .replace("name: \"Add\"", "name: \"Save profile\"")
            .replace("beginForm(\"add\", { title }", "beginForm(\"saveProfile\", { name: title }");
    } else if template == Some("chat") {
        proof = proof.replace("    const task =", r#"    const viewer = await page.evaluate(() => window.__snapback4Witness.viewer());
    const seeded = await fetch(`${dev.url}/seed`, { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify([
      { table: "conversations", row: { id: "proof-room", title: "Proof room", creatorId: viewer } },
      { table: "memberships", row: { id: "proof-member", conversationId: "proof-room", principal: viewer, role: "admin", joinedAt: Date.now() } }
    ]) });
    assert.ok(seeded.ok, await seeded.text());
    await page.getByText("Proof room", { exact: true }).click();
    const task ="#)
            .replace("page.getByLabel(\"Task\", { exact: true })", "page.getByPlaceholder(\"Message\", { exact: true })")
            .replace("name: \"Add\"", "name: \"Send\"")
            .replace("beginForm(\"add\", { title }", "beginForm(\"send\", { conversationId: \"proof-room\", body: title }");
        // Chat receipts echo drafts. Restrict evidence to its message display.
        if web { proof = proof.replace("renderedText(page, new RegExp(title))", "renderedText(page, new RegExp(title), { within: page.getByRole(\"region\", { name: \"Thread\" }) })"); }
    }
    let scope = match (template, web) {
        (Some("social"), _) => "page.getByTestId(\"profile-results\")",
        (Some("chat"), true) => "page.getByRole(\"region\", { name: \"Thread\" })",
        (Some("chat"), false) => "page.getByTestId(\"thread-results\")",
        (_, true) => "page.getByRole(\"list\")",
        (_, false) => "page.getByTestId(\"todo-results\")",
    };
    proof = proof.replace("RESULTS_SCOPE", scope);
    proof = proof.replace("BUILD_SCRIPT", if web { "build" } else { "build:web" });
    proof
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#10-wave-20-loop-repairs-dx5 — a rendered starter is not an application write proof.
pub(super) fn frontend_scaffold(web: bool) -> String {
    let source = include_str!("frontend-proof.mjs").replace("// SHARED_IMPORT", "").replace("// LABELLED_CONTROLS", "");
    let proof = frontend_proof(&source, None, web);
    let (setup, _) = proof.split_once("    const task =").expect("frontend proof's write section");
    // A public custom schema need not invent identity just to prove rendering.
    let setup = setup.replace(r#"} else {
      assert.ok(personas.length, "declare identity or personas before proving the starter");"#, "} else if (personas.length) {")
        .replace(r#"    await page.getByRole("button", { name: /^(Sign out|Switch persona)$/, exact: true }).waitFor();"#, r#"    if (identity.guests || identity.password || personas.length) await page.getByRole("button", { name: /^(Sign out|Switch persona)$/, exact: true }).waitFor();"#);
    format!("{setup}    assert.ok((await page.locator('body').innerText()).trim(), 'frontend rendered no content');\n    // Add a real UI write and assert its SDK receipt and fresh rendered result here.\n    throw Error('E_FRONTEND_SCENARIO: frontend rendered; no app-specific write scenario yet. Edit scripts/frontend-proof.mjs and replace this refusal with your write assertions.');\n  }});\n}} finally {{ await cleanup(() => web?.stop(), () => dev.stop()); }}\n")
}

fn previous_frontend(dir: &std::path::Path, web: bool) -> Option<Option<&'static str>> {
    super::proof_advice::frontend_template(dir, web).filter(|(version,_)| matches!(*version,"0.4.28"|"0.4.29"|"0.4.30"|"0.4.31"|"0.4.32")).map(|(_,template)|template)
}

/// Adapt adds missing proof infrastructure, preserving files and script names
/// already owned by the application. It never runs a package manager.
pub(super) fn plan(dir: &std::path::Path, own: &super::OwnSchema, enable_day2: bool, refresh_default_frontend: bool) -> std::io::Result<Vec<(&'static str, String)>> {
    let web = dir.join("src/App.tsx").is_file();
    let router = dir.join("app/_layout.tsx").is_file();
    if !web && !dir.join("App.tsx").is_file() && !router { return Ok(Vec::new()); }
    let shape = super::starter::Shape { title: super::title(dir), password: own.identity.is_some_and(|(p, g)| p && !g),
        personas: own.identity.is_none().then(|| own.personas.clone()).filter(|p| !p.is_empty()),
        body: if super::adapt::fits_todos(own) { super::starter::Body::Todos } else { super::starter::Body::Home(None) }, offline: own.offline.clone() };
    let path = dir.join("package.json");
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path)?;
    let mut package: Value = serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(std::io::Error::other)?;
    let router = router && !web && package["main"] == "expo-router/entry";
    if !web && !dir.join("App.tsx").is_file() && !router { return Ok(Vec::new()); }
    let original = package.clone();
    let day2_text = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("scripts/day2-proof.mjs")).ok();
        "fix": if found.is_empty() { serde_json::Value::Null } else { "snapback4 init --adapt".into() },
    })
}

/// `snapback4 init --adapt [dir]`.
pub(super) fn adapt(dir: &Path, dry_run: bool, apply: bool, day2: bool) -> i32 {
    if day2 { println!("{}", if dry_run || !apply { snapback4_lang::guide::card("day2").unwrap().to_owned() } else { crate::guide_seen::render_in(dir, "day2", snapback4_lang::guide::card("day2").unwrap(), false) }); }
    let own = match own_schema(dir) {
        Ok(Some(own)) => own,
        Ok(None) => { eprintln!("init --adapt: no snapback/*.q in {}; nothing to adapt to (run `snapback4 init --web` or `--expo` first)", dir.display()); return 1; }
        Err(error) => { eprintln!("init: {error}"); return 2; }
    };
    let (found, mut advice, modified) = plan(dir);
    advice.extend(super::proof_advice::report(dir));
    let mut proof = match super::proof::plan(dir, &own, day2, default_frontend_ready(dir, &found, &modified)) {
        Ok(proof) => proof,
        Err(error) => { eprintln!("init --adapt proof: {error}"); return 2; }
    };
    if day2 {
        let sources = own.files.iter().map(|name| std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("snapback").join(name)).map(|text|(name.clone(),text))).collect::<std::io::Result<Vec<_>>>();
        match sources.and_then(|sources|super::frozen::files(dir,&sources)) {
            Ok(files) => proof.extend(files.into_iter().filter(|(name,_)| !proof.iter().any(|(existing,_)|existing==name)).collect::<Vec<_>>()),
            Err(error) => { eprintln!("init --adapt frozen client: {error}"); return 2; }
        }
    }
    if !found.is_empty() || !proof.is_empty() {
        println!("init --adapt: planned changes (only unmodified starter files):");
        for change in &found { println!("  {} {} — {}", if change.rewrite.is_some() { "rewrite" } else { "remove" }, change.path, change.why); }
    }
    for line in super::proof_advice::legacy_differences(dir, &proof) { println!("init --adapt: {line}"); }
    for (path, body) in &proof {
        if path.starts_with("versions/") {
            println!("  add {path} — capture current snapback/*.q and contemporary fixtures; legacy flat sources stay unchanged");
            continue;
        }
        let exists = dir.join(path).exists();
        println!("  {} {} — {} proof infrastructure", if *path == "package.json" { "extend" } else if exists { "replace" } else { "add" }, path, if exists { "inherited" } else { "missing" });
        if *path == "package.json" {
            let old: serde_json::Value = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join(path)).ok().and_then(|text| serde_json::from_str(&text).ok()).unwrap_or_default();
            let new: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(body).unwrap();
            for group in ["scripts", "devDependencies"] {
                let keys = old[group].as_object().into_iter().flat_map(|o|o.keys()).chain(new[group].as_object().into_iter().flat_map(|o|o.keys())).collect::<std::collections::BTreeSet<_>>();
                for key in keys { if old[group][key] != new[group][key] { println!("    {group}.{key}: {} → {}", old[group][key], new[group][key]); } }
            }
            if old.pointer("/snapback4/starter/day2") != new.pointer("/snapback4/starter/day2") {
                println!("    snapback4.starter.day2: {} → {}", old["snapback4"]["starter"]["day2"], new["snapback4"]["starter"]["day2"]);
            }
            println!("    serialize package.json (including script key ordering)");
        }
    }
    if !modified.is_empty() { println!("init --adapt: application-owned or modified files stay unchanged: {}", modified.join(", ")); }
    if dry_run || !apply {
        for line in advice { println!("init --adapt: {line}"); }
        if !fits_todos(&own) && [false,true].into_iter().any(|web| super::proof_advice::frontend_template(dir, web).is_some_and(|(_,template)|template.is_none())) { println!("init --adapt: no write scenario: add an app-specific frontend proof with snapback4/proof after replacing Home"); }
        println!("init --adapt: preview only; no files changed. Apply with:");
        println!("snapback4 init{} --adapt{} --apply", if dir == Path::new(".") { String::new() } else { format!(" '{}'", dir.to_string_lossy().replace('\'', "'\"'\"'")) }, if day2 { " --day2" } else { "" });
        return 0;
    }
    if let Err(error) = super::proof::apply(dir, &proof) { eprintln!("init --adapt proof: {error}"); return 2; }
    let mut removed = Vec::new();
    let mut rewritten = Vec::new();
    for leftover in &found {
        let path = dir.join(leftover.path);
        let result = match &leftover.rewrite {
            Some(text) => super::replace_file(&path, text).map(|()| rewritten.push(leftover.path)),
            None => std::fs::remove_file(&path).map(|()| removed.push(leftover.path)),
        };
        if let Err(error) = result { eprintln!("init --adapt: {}: {error}", path.display()); return 2; }
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — adapt removes the files it names, never a directory: the next step writes fixtures/<table>.jsonl.
    if found.is_empty() && proof.is_empty() { println!("init --adapt: nothing to adapt — no unmodified starter file conflicts with your snapback/*.q"); }
    if !removed.is_empty() {
        let kept = if removed.iter().any(|path| path.starts_with("fixtures/")) { "; fixtures/ stays for your own rows" } else { "" };
        println!("init --adapt: removed {} (unmodified starter files{kept}; write your own as fixtures/<table>.jsonl and fixtures/test.cases.jsonl, `snapback4 guide testing`)", removed.join(" and "));
    }
    if !rewritten.is_empty() {
        let why = found.iter().find(|leftover| leftover.rewrite.is_some()).map(|leftover| leftover.why.as_str()).unwrap_or_default();
        println!("init --adapt: rewrote {} — {why}", rewritten.join(" and "));
    }
    if found.iter().any(|leftover| leftover.rewrite.as_deref().is_some_and(|text| text.contains("function Home("))) {
        println!("init --adapt: next: `snapback4 check` writes snapback/generated/api.ts for the Home screen; replace Home with your own screens (`snapback4 guide client`)");
    }
    for line in advice { println!("init --adapt: {line}"); }
    0
}

    pub fn build_retained_views(
        &self,
        schema: &Schema,

        all_releases: bool,
        mut ephemeral: impl FnMut(&str),
    ) -> Result<()> {
        let conn = &self.conn;

        let mut backing = BTreeSet::new();

                continue;
            }

                    "E_SQL_INTERNAL",
                    format!(
                        "{name} declares {STATE}, {SOURCE} or {COLUMNS}; retained inspection reserves those result columns"

                .query_row("SELECT num FROM _catalog WHERE name = ?1", [name], |row| {
                    row.get(0)
                })
                .map_err(store_error)?;
            let private = format!("{PRIVATE}{number}");
            let projected = |data: &str, defaults: bool| {
                let mut columns = vec!["id".to_owned()];

                    if column == "id" {

                    }
                    let tag = scalar_tag(kind);
                    let path = format!("$.Row.{}{tag}", ident(column));
                    let mut value = format!("json_extract({data}, {})", literal(&path));
                    if table.maintained.contains_key(column) {
                        value = format!("coalesce({value}, 0)");
                    }

                        && !kind.is_optional()

                    {

                            "json_extract({}, {})",
                            literal(&crate::value::canonical_json(default)),
                            literal(&format!("${tag}"))
                        );
                        let field = literal(&format!("$.Row.{}", ident(column)));
                        value = format!(
                            "CASE WHEN json_type({data}, {field}) IS NULL THEN {default} ELSE {value} END"
                        );
                    }
                    columns.push(format!(
                        "{} AS {}",
                        if tag.is_empty() {
                            format!("nullif({value}, 'Null')")
                        } else {
                            value
                        },
                        ident(column)
                    ));
                }
                columns
            };
            let columns = projected("data", true);
            let provided = |data: &str| {
                let values = table
                    .columns
                    .keys()
                    .filter(|column| column.as_str() != "id" && !table.engine_field(column))
                    .map(|column| {
                        format!(
                            "CASE WHEN json_type({data},{}) IS NOT NULL THEN {} ELSE '' END",
                            literal(&format!("$.Row.{}", ident(column))),
                            literal(&format!("{},", serde_json::to_string(column).unwrap()))
                        )
                    })
                    .collect::<Vec<_>>();
                if values.is_empty() {
                    "'[]'".to_owned()
                } else {
                    format!("'[' || rtrim({}, ',') || ']'", values.join(" || "))
                }
            };
            let mut source_views = String::new();
            let backing_sql = if dormant || all_releases {
                let active_view = format!("{private}_active");
                let table_view = format!("{private}_table");
                let narrowed_view = format!("{private}_narrowed");
                let cells_view = format!("{private}_cells");
                source_views = format!(
                    "CREATE TEMP VIEW {} AS SELECT id,data FROM main.r WHERE t={number}; CREATE TEMP VIEW {} AS SELECT id,data FROM main._dormant_rows WHERE t={number}; CREATE TEMP VIEW {} AS SELECT id,data FROM main._dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t={number}; CREATE TEMP VIEW {} AS SELECT t,id,column_name,data FROM main._dormant_values WHERE t={number};",

                parts.push(format!("SELECT {},'dormant-row' AS {STATE},'narrowed' AS {SOURCE},{} AS {COLUMNS} FROM temp.{} d",projected("d.data",false).join(", "),provided("d.data"),ident(&narrowed_view)));

            views.insert(name.to_ascii_lowercase());
            backing.insert(private);
        }
        self.authorize(views, backing)?;
        Ok(())
    }
const COLUMNS: &str = "_snapback_columns";
fn scalar_tag(kind: &ColumnType) -> &'static str {
    match kind.required() {
        ColumnType::Id | ColumnType::Principal | ColumnType::Ref { .. } => ".Id",
        ColumnType::Text { .. } | ColumnType::Enum { .. } => ".String",
        ColumnType::Int
        | ColumnType::IntRange { .. }
        | ColumnType::Time
        | ColumnType::Decimal { .. }
        | ColumnType::Money { .. } => ".Int",
        ColumnType::Bool => ".Bool",
        ColumnType::Bytes { .. } => ".Bytes",
        _ => "",
    }
}

#[derive(serde::Deserialize)]
struct RetainedColumn {
    kind: ColumnType,
    default: Option<ColumnDefault>,
    authoritative: bool,
}

impl Inspection {
    pub fn retained_schema(&self, mut schema: Schema) -> Result<Schema> {
        let conn = &self.conn;
        let mut historical = BTreeMap::<String, Table>::new();
        let mut statement=conn.prepare("SELECT table_name,definition FROM _dormant_tables UNION SELECT c.name,n.definition FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=n.t ORDER BY 1").map_err(store_error)?;
        let rows = statement
            .query_map([], |row| {
                Ok((row.get::<_, String>(0)?, row.get::<_, String>(1)?))
            })
            .map_err(store_error)?;
        for row in rows {
            let (name, definition) = row.map_err(store_error)?;
            let table: Table =
                serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e| ("E_SCHEMA", e.to_string()))?;
            historical.entry(name).or_insert(table);
        }
        for (name, old) in historical {
            if let Some(table) = schema.tables.get_mut(&name) {
                for (column, kind) in &old.columns {
                    if !old.engine_field(column) {
                        table.columns.entry(column.clone()).or_insert(kind.clone());
                        if let Some(default) = old.defaults.get(column) {
                            table
                                .defaults
                                .entry(column.clone())
                                .or_insert(default.clone());
                        }
                    }
                }
            } else {
                schema.tables.insert(name, old);
            }
        }
        let mut statement=conn.prepare("SELECT table_name,column_name,definition FROM _dormant_columns ORDER BY table_name,column_name").map_err(store_error)?;
        let rows = statement
            .query_map([], |row| {
                Ok((
                    row.get::<_, String>(0)?,
                    row.get::<_, String>(1)?,
                    row.get::<_, String>(2)?,
                ))
            })
            .map_err(store_error)?;
        for row in rows {
            let (table, column, definition) = row.map_err(store_error)?;
            let retained: RetainedColumn =
                serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e| ("E_SCHEMA", e.to_string()))?;
            if !retained.authoritative {
                continue;
            }
            if let Some(target) = schema.tables.get_mut(&table) {
                target
                    .columns
                    .entry(column.clone())
                    .or_insert(retained.kind);
                if let Some(default) = retained.default {
                    target.defaults.entry(column).or_insert(default);
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(schema)
    }
}

/** Administrative snapshot of active and retained rows, merged once per id; bypasses application read rules.
 * The CLI reads adopted schema and rows in one transaction; ephemeral tables refuse.
 * @throws E_PROOF_SNAPSHOT for CLI refusal or malformed/incomplete inspection output. */
export async function snapshotAll<Row = Record<string, unknown>>(table: string, options: ProofOptions & { release?: string } = {}): Promise<Row[]> {
  if (!/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]*$/.test(table)) throw Error("E_PROOF_SNAPSHOT: supply one table name");
  const result = await cli(["data", table, "--all", "--json", ...(options.release ? ["--release", options.release] : [])], options);
  if (result.code !== 0) throw Error(`E_PROOF_SNAPSHOT: ${result.stderr.trim()}`);
  let value;
  try { value = JSON.parse(result.stdout); } catch { throw Error("E_PROOF_SNAPSHOT: inspection returned invalid JSON"); }
  if (!Array.isArray(value.rows) || value.more !== false || value.limit !== null) throw Error("E_PROOF_SNAPSHOT: inspection did not certify all rows");
  return value.rows as Row[];
}

// Publication extractors deliberately consume every bounded reference section.
// Builders should read one section at a time; this is a programmatic corpus check.
import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
export function guideReference(cli, card, options = {}) {
  const invoke = args => {
    const result = spawnSync(process.execPath, [cli,"guide",card,...args], {encoding:"utf8",timeout:30_000,maxBuffer:4*1024*1024,...options});
    if (result.status !== 0) throw Error(result.stderr + result.stdout);
    return result.stdout;
  };
  const index = invoke(["--sections"]);
  const numbers = [...index.matchAll(/^\s*(\d+)\. /gm)].map(match=>match[1]);
  if (!numbers.length) return invoke(["--full","--repeat"]);
  const pieces = [invoke(["--section","opening","--repeat"])];
  for (const number of numbers) pieces.push(invoke(["--section",number,"--repeat"]));
  return pieces.join("\n\n");
}
