//! Reserved words: Quarry keywords cannot name a column or a binding. The
//! refusal names the word at its declaration, before a later use fails.

use crate::diag::{Diagnostic, Site};

/// Words that begin a table line (`by`, `read <-`, `after insert`, …).
pub(crate) const TABLE_WORDS: &[&str] = &[
    "after", "by", "cap", "delete", "deliver", "ephemeral", "expire", "immutable", "insert", "online",
    "public", "read", "retain", "search", "sync", "unique", "update",
];

/// Words a statement or expression reads as syntax, so a parameter, local or
/// loop variable of that name cannot be used (or silently means the keyword).
// @ref LLP 3000.036.002#9 — commit/new/now/viewer are contextual builtins, so declarations cannot shadow them.
pub(crate) const BINDING_WORDS: &[&str] = &[
    "and", "as", "break", "commit", "continue", "created", "delete", "device", "elif", "else", "emit",
    "exists", "false", "for", "if", "insert", "merge", "new", "not", "now", "null", "oblivious", "or",
    "require", "return", "true", "update", "upsert", "viewer", "walk", "while",
];

fn rename(word: &str) -> String {
    match word {
        "read" => "isRead".into(),
        "by" => "author".into(),
        "after" => "afterAt".into(),
        "update" => "change".into(),
        "delete" => "removal".into(),
        "insert" => "addition".into(),
        "public" => "isPublic".into(),
        "unique" => "isUnique".into(),
        "sync" => "synced".into(),
        "cap" => "capacity".into(),
        "expire" => "expiresAt".into(),
        "not" => "startsAt".into(),
        "online" => "isOnline".into(),
        "search" => "searchText".into(),
        "retain" => "retained".into(),
        "immutable" => "locked".into(),
        "ephemeral" => "transient".into(),
        "deliver" => "delivery".into(),
        "walk" => "outing".into(),
        "viewer" => "who".into(),
        "now" => "at".into(),
        "commit" => "committedAt".into(),
        "new" => "newId".into(),
        other => format!("{other}Value"),
    }
}

pub(crate) fn column(site: &Site, word: &str) -> Diagnostic {
    let name = rename(word);
    Diagnostic::new(site, "E_QUARRY_RESERVED",
        format!("`{word}` is a reserved word: it begins a table line (`{}`), so it cannot name a column", usage(word)),
        format!("rename the column, for example `{name}:`, and use `.{name}` in rules and queries (reserved words: snapback4 why E_QUARRY_RESERVED)"))
}

pub(crate) fn binding(site: &Site, word: &str) -> Diagnostic {
    let name = rename(word);
    Diagnostic::new(site, "E_QUARRY_RESERVED",
        format!("`{word}` is a reserved word, so it cannot name a parameter, local or loop variable"),
        format!("rename it, for example `{name}`, at this declaration and at each use (reserved words: snapback4 why E_QUARRY_RESERVED)"))
}

pub(crate) fn repeated_identity(
    site: &Site,
    first: (bool, bool),
    first_site: &Site,
    repeated: (bool, bool),
) -> Diagnostic {
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.002#9 — identity is project-wide and repeated declarations must name both method sets.
    let methods = |(password, guests)| match (password, guests) {
        (true, true) => "password and guests",
        (true, false) => "password",
        (false, true) => "guests",
        (false, false) => "no methods",
    };
    Diagnostic::new(
        site,
        "E_QUARRY_IDENTITY_DUPLICATE",
        format!("`use identity` is project-wide: {} already declared at {first_site}; this repeated declaration names {}", methods(first), methods(repeated)),
        "keep one declaration: `use identity` (password and guests), `use identity password`, or `use identity guests`",
    )
    .guide("production")
}

    10	    let room = FULL_BYTES.saturating_sub(title.len() + 350);
    11	    let mut out = format!("{title}\n\nRead one section for your change. `guide {name} --full` lists sections; --repeat reprints a selection.\n\n");
    12	    let mut heading = "Opening".to_owned();
    13	    let mut page = String::new();
    14	    let mut part = 1;
    15	    let flush = |out: &mut String, page: &mut String, heading: &str, part: usize| {
    16	        if page.is_empty() { return; }

    19	    };
    20	    let mut fenced = false;

    23	    for line in card.lines().skip(1) {

    25	            if !fenced { prepared.push('\n'); }

    31	    }
    32	    for block in reading_blocks(&prepared) {
    33	        if block.starts_with("## ") || block.starts_with("### ") {
    34	            flush(&mut out, &mut page, &heading, part);

fn native_images_survive_optional_missing_inputs_and_constant_backfills() {
 for optional in [false,true] {
  let old="table items:\n  n: int ?\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
  let next=old.replace("  n: int ?","  derived: int")+if optional {"backfill items derive(n: int ?):\n  {derived:(n ?? 0) * 2}\n"}else{"backfill items derive():\n  {derived:0}\n"};
  let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"native","derived":9}));
  adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","native","derived"),Value::Int(9));
  adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",3).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","native","derived"),Value::Int(9));
 }
}
#[test]
fn invalid_present_inputs_never_qualify_as_native_missing_inputs() {
 let v2="table items:\n  n: bool\n  derived: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let old=v2.replace("  derived: int\n","");
 let next=v2.to_owned()+"backfill items derive(n: int, missing: int ?):\n  {derived:n + (missing ?? 0)}\n";
 let mut e=engine(v2);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":true,"derived":9}));adopt(&mut e,&old,1).unwrap();
 let error=adopt(&mut e,&next,3).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("historical input items.n"),"{error}");
}
#[test]
fn original_absence_survives_later_defaults_until_a_meaningful_input_edit() {
 let old="table items:\n  label: text <=10\n  public 'fixture'\n  update <- allow\n  online only\n";
 let fill="backfill items derive(n: int ?):\n  {derived:n ?? 0}\n";
 let next=old.replace("  label:","  derived: int\n  label:")+fill;
 let later=next.replace("  label:","  n: int default 100\n  label:")+"mutation edit(n: int):\n  update items['a'] {n}\nmutation label():\n  update items['a'] {label:'changed'}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","label":"old"}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&later,3).unwrap();write(&mut e,"label");
 adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",4).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&later,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","derived"),Value::Int(0));
 for n in [200,100] {let r=e.mutate("edit",None,Context::new("alice",std::collections::BTreeMap::from([("n".into(),Value::Int(n))]))).unwrap();assert!(matches!(r.state,WriteState::Sent{..}),"{r:?}");}
 adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",4).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&later,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","derived"),Value::Int(100));
}

#[test]
fn a_new_declaration_name_does_not_inherit_another_native_definition() {
 let old="table items:\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  public","  derived: int\n  public")+"backfill items zero():\n  {derived:0}\n";
 let newer=next.replace("items zero()","items one()").replace("derived:0","derived:1");
 let mut e=engine(old);adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"native","derived":9}));adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&newer,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","native","derived"),Value::Int(1));
}

#[test]
//! SQLite read-only inspection connection and generated application views.
// @ref LLP 3000.009.001.000#32-the-rule — inspection SQL stays inside the adapter.
use super::{Store, Tx};
use crate::inspection::{
    CATALOG_HINT, InspectionCatalog, InspectionResult as Result, PRIVATE, internal,
};
use crate::schema::{ColumnDefault, ColumnType, Schema, Table};
use rusqlite::{
    Connection, OpenFlags, OptionalExtension,
    hooks::{AuthAction, AuthContext, Authorization},
    types::ValueRef,
};
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use std::{
    collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet},
    path::Path,
    sync::{
        Arc,
        atomic::{AtomicU8, Ordering},
    },
};

pub struct Inspection {
    conn: Connection,
    denied: Arc<AtomicU8>,
}
pub use crate::inspection::{InspectionColumnKind, InspectionRows};
impl Inspection {
    pub fn open(path: &Path) -> Result<Self> {
        let conn = Connection::open_with_flags(path, OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_READ_ONLY)
            .map_err(store_error)?;
        Ok(Self {
            conn,
            denied: Arc::new(AtomicU8::new(0)),
        })
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#11-wave-22-loop-repairs-dx6 — inspection refuses future formats before exposing any records.
    pub fn begin_snapshot(&self) -> Result<()> {
        self.conn.execute_batch("BEGIN DEFERRED").map_err(store_error)?;
        super::Store::check_version(&self.conn).map_err(|error| ("E_SCHEMA_CAPABILITY", error.to_string()))
    }
    pub fn build_views(&self, schema: &Schema, ephemeral: impl FnMut(&str)) -> Result<()> {
        self.build_retained_views(schema, false, false, ephemeral)
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#19.4 — active and retained records share one snapshot and authorizer.
    pub fn build_retained_views(
        &self,
        schema: &Schema,
        dormant: bool,
        all_releases: bool,
        mut ephemeral: impl FnMut(&str),
    ) -> Result<()> {
        let conn = &self.conn;
        let mut views = BTreeSet::new();
        let mut backing = BTreeSet::new();
        for (name, table) in schema.visible_tables() {
            if table.ephemeral.is_some() {
                ephemeral(name);
                continue;
            }
            if internal(name) {
                continue;
            }
            if (dormant || all_releases)
                && table
                    .columns
                    .keys()
                    .any(|column| matches!(column.as_str(), STATE | SOURCE | COLUMNS))
            {
                return Err((
                    "E_SQL_INTERNAL",
                    format!(
                        "{name} declares {STATE}, {SOURCE} or {COLUMNS}; retained inspection reserves those result columns"
                    ),
                ));
            }
            let number: i64 = conn
                .query_row("SELECT num FROM _catalog WHERE name = ?1", [name], |row| {
                    row.get(0)
                })
        return ExitCode::from(1);
    }
    // Release-directory inspection can create SQLite WAL bookkeeping too.
    // Admit its directory before any administrative reader opens the database.
    // Token and lifecycle consumers perform this same guard at their own entry.
    if !store_config::selected(&options) && matches!(verb, "run" | "why" | "data" | "sql" | "doctor")
        && let Some(directory) = options.flags.get("release")
        && let Err(error) = verbs::existing_deployment(std::path::Path::new(directory)) {
        eprintln!("{verb}: {error}");
        return ExitCode::from(2);
    }
    let stateless_why = verb == "why" && options.positionals.first().is_some_and(|code| guide::explain_code(code).is_some());
    if !stateless_why && store_config::selected(&options) && matches!(verb, "rollback"|"prune"|"releases"|"stop"|"run"|"why"|"data"|"token") {
        if let Err(error) = store_config::StoreConfig::resolve(&options) { eprintln!("{error}"); return ExitCode::from(2); }
        eprintln!("E_STORE_UNSUPPORTED: this operator command is outside the admitted store profile");
        return ExitCode::from(1);
    }
    let code = match verb {
        "--version" | "-V" | "version" => {
            println!("snapback4 {}", env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"));
            0
        }
        "new" if !options.flags.contains_key("template") => {
            eprintln!("Choose a template: --template social, or --template chat with --web or --expo\nusage: {}\nFor a small starter use snapback4 init <dir> --web (or --expo); for schema-only setup use snapback4 init <dir>.", cli_flags::synopsis("new"));
            2
        }
        "init" | "new" => verbs::init(&options),
        "upgrade" => verbs::upgrade::run(&args[1..]),
        "deploy" => verbs::deploy(&options),
        "rollback" => verbs::rollback(&options),
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum InspectionColumnKind {
    Plain,
    Bool,
    Media,
}

fn narrowing_hides_reference_closure_and_rebuilds_unchanged_counts_and_indexes() {
    let wide="table parents:\n  status: enum('live', 'new')\n  public\n  sync public\ntable children:\n  parent: parents\n  immutable parent\n  public\n  sync public\ntable leaves:\n  child: children\n  immutable child\n  public\n  sync public\nview counts: count children by .parent\nquery total(p: parents):\n  return counts[p]\n";
    let old=wide.replace("enum('live', 'new')","enum('live')");
    let mut e=engine(wide);
    seed(&mut e,"parents",serde_json::json!({"id":"p","status":"new"}));
    seed(&mut e,"children",serde_json::json!({"id":"c","parent":"p"}));
    seed(&mut e,"leaves",serde_json::json!({"id":"l","child":"c"}));
    let total=|e:&mut Engine|e.query("total",Context::new("alice",[("p".into(),Value::Id("p".into()))].into())).unwrap().data;
    assert_eq!(total(&mut e),Value::Int(1));
    activate(&mut e,&old,2).unwrap();
    for table in ["parents","children","leaves"] {assert_eq!(e.store().count(table).unwrap(),0, "{table}");}
    assert_eq!(total(&mut e),Value::Int(0));
    assert!(e.snapshot("alice").unwrap().0.iter().all(|event| !["parents","children","leaves"].contains(&event.table.as_str())));
    activate(&mut e,wide,3).unwrap();
    for table in ["parents","children","leaves"] {assert_eq!(e.store().count(table).unwrap(),1, "{table}");}
    assert_eq!(total(&mut e),Value::Int(1));
    let status=e.store_mut().release_status(0,None,None).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(status["events"][1]["summary"]["narrowed_retained"],3);
    assert_eq!(status["events"][2]["summary"]["narrowed_restored"],3);
}

#[test]
fn too_short_old_writes_are_dormant_on_forward_and_restored_on_rollback() {
    let loose="table codes:\n  code: text 4..12\n  unique code\n  public\n  sync public\n";
    let tight=loose.replace("4..12","6..12");
    let mut e=engine(loose);
    seed(&mut e,"codes",serde_json::json!({"id":"short","code":"four"}));
    seed(&mut e,"codes",serde_json::json!({"id":"long","code":"longer"}));
    activate(&mut e,&tight,2).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(e.store().count("codes").unwrap(),1);
    // Primary identity and references cannot overwrite or acquire hidden rows.
    let result=e.seed([("codes".into(),wire::row_from_json(&e.schema().tables["codes"],&serde_json::json!({"id":"short","code":"newer"})).unwrap())]);
    assert!(matches!(result.unwrap_err().code().as_str(),"E_RULE"|"E_CONSTRAINT"));
    activate(&mut e,loose,1).unwrap();
    let rows=e.store().rows("codes").unwrap();
    assert_eq!(rows.len(),2);
    assert!(rows.iter().any(|r|r.field("code").unwrap()==&Value::String("four".into())));
    activate(&mut e,&tight,2).unwrap();
    let preview=e.store_mut().prune_dormant(false,None).unwrap();
    assert!(preview["items"].as_array().unwrap().iter().any(|i|i["key"]=="rows:codes"));
    e.store_mut().prune_dormant(true,None).unwrap();
    activate(&mut e,loose,1).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(e.store().count("codes").unwrap(),1);
}

#[test]
fn dormant_row_reference_never_recaptures_a_deleted_parent_lifetime() {
    let wide="table parents:\n  public\ntable children:\n  parent: parents\n  status: enum('live','new')\n  public\n";
    let old=wide.replace("enum('live','new')","enum('live')");
    let mut e=engine(wide);

    seed(&mut e,"children",serde_json::json!({"id":"c","parent":"p","status":"new"}));
    activate(&mut e,&old,2).unwrap();
    {let table=e.schema().tables["parents"].clone();let tx=e.store_mut().transaction().unwrap();let row=tx.get("parents","p").unwrap().unwrap();tx.delete("parents",&table,&row).unwrap();tx.commit().unwrap();}
    seed(&mut e,"parents",serde_json::json!({"id":"p"}));
    activate(&mut e,wide,3).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(e.store().count("children").unwrap(),0);
    assert_eq!(e.store_mut().dormant_inventory(None,100).unwrap().iter().find(|i|i.key=="rows:children").unwrap().rows,1);
}

fn restoration_into_a_narrower_table_or_column_retains_source_values_and_definition() {
    let wide="table rows:\n  status: enum('live','new') default 'live'\n  public\nquery version():\n  return 1\n";
    let narrow=wide.replace("enum('live','new')","enum('live')");
    for retired in ["query version():\n  return 1\n".to_string(),wide.replace("  status: enum('live','new') default 'live'\n","")] {
        let mut e=engine(wide);
        seed(&mut e,"rows",serde_json::json!({"id":"r","status":"new"}));
        activate(&mut e,&retired,2).unwrap();
        activate(&mut e,&narrow,3).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(e.store().count("rows").unwrap(),0);
        let status=e.store_mut().release_status(0,None,None).unwrap();let event=status["events"].as_array().unwrap().last().unwrap();
        let items=e.store_mut().release_status(0,None,event["id"].as_u64()).unwrap();
        let restored=items["items"].as_array().unwrap().iter().find(|i|i["key"]==if retired.contains("table rows") {"column:rows.status"} else {"table:rows"}).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(restored["restored_rows"],0);
        assert_ne!(restored["action"],"restored");
        // Whole-table reintroduction also restores its byId index definition.
        assert_eq!(event["summary"]["restored_items"],if retired.contains("table rows") {0} else {1});
        // Retiring the field again must preserve the original stored type/value.
        activate(&mut e,&retired,4).unwrap();
        activate(&mut e,wide,5).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(e.store().rows("rows").unwrap()[0].field("status").unwrap(),&Value::String("new".into()));
    }
}

#[test]
fn tentative_restoration_with_no_visibility_delta_keeps_generation_and_cursors() {

    let old=wide.replace("enum('live','new')","enum('live')");

    seed(&mut e,"children",serde_json::json!({"id":"c","parent":"p"}));
    activate(&mut e,&old,2).unwrap();
    let generation=e.generation();
    activate(&mut e,&old,2).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(e.generation(),generation);
    assert_eq!(e.store().count("parents").unwrap(),0);
    assert_eq!(e.store().count("children").unwrap(),0);
}

//! Privileged, bounded population evidence for adoption refusals.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#3-older-writes-and-incompatible-values — count all affected rows, retain at most five samples.
use super::*;
use crate::schema::{Constraint, materialize_optional_fields};

#[derive(Default)]
pub(crate) struct Samples {
    pub count: u64,
    ids: Vec<String>,
    values: Vec<String>,
    last_id: Option<String>,
}
pub(crate) fn bounded(text: &str) -> String {
    if text.len() <= 256 {
        return text.into();
    }
    let mut end = 256;
    while !text.is_char_boundary(end) {
        end -= 1;
    }
    format!("{}… [truncated]", &text[..end])
}

pub(crate) fn value_sample(value: &Value) -> String {
    struct Prefix(Vec<u8>);
    impl std::io::Write for Prefix {
        fn write(&mut self, bytes: &[u8]) -> std::io::Result<usize> {
            let n = bytes.len().min(256 - self.0.len());
            self.0.extend_from_slice(&bytes[..n]);
            if n < bytes.len() {
                return Err(std::io::Error::other("sample truncated"));
            }
            Ok(n)
        }
        fn flush(&mut self) -> std::io::Result<()> {
            Ok(())
        }
    }
    if let Value::Bytes(bytes) = value {
        return format!("{} bytes", bytes.len());
    }
    let mut prefix = Prefix(Vec::with_capacity(256));
    let truncated = serde_json::to_writer(&mut prefix, &crate::wire::Plain(value)).is_err();
    let mut text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&prefix.0).into_owned();
    if truncated {
        text.push_str("… [truncated]")
    }
    text
}
impl Samples {
    pub fn add(&mut self, id: &str, value: &str) {
        if self.last_id.as_deref() != Some(id) {
            self.count += 1;
            if self.ids.len() < 5 {
                self.ids.push(bounded(id));
            }
            self.last_id = Some(id.into());
        }
        let value = bounded(value);
        if self.values.len() < 5 && !self.values.contains(&value) {
            self.values.push(value);
        }
    }
    pub fn describe(&self) -> String {
        format!(
            "{} affected rows; sample ids [{}]; values/keys [{}]",
            self.count,
            self.ids.join(", "),
            self.values.join(", ")
        )
    }
}

pub(super) fn candidate(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    schema: &Schema,
    previous: Option<&Schema>,
    complete: bool,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    for (name, table) in schema
        .tables
        .iter()
        .filter(|(n, t)| t.ephemeral.is_none() && schema.maintain_for(n).is_none())
    {
        if previous.and_then(|p| p.table(name)) == Some(table) {
            continue;
        }
        let mut bad = Samples::default();
        super::activation::walk(tx, tx.num(name)?, |id, mut row| {
            if let Value::Row(f) | Value::Map(f) = &mut row {
                f.retain(|c, _| table.columns.contains_key(c));
            }
            materialize_optional_fields(&mut row, table);
            let validation = if complete {
                crate::engine::validate::validate_row(schema, name, &row)
            } else {
                crate::engine::validate::validate_adoption_input(schema, name, &row)
            };
            if let Err(error) = validation {
                let mut detailed = false;
                for (column, kind) in &table.columns {
                    if (complete || !table.engine_field(column))
                        && crate::engine::validate::validate_column(
                            name,
                            column,
                            kind,
                            row.field(column).ok(),
                        )
                        .is_err()
                    {
                        let value = row
                            .field(column)
                            .map(value_sample)
                            .unwrap_or_else(|_| "<missing>".into());
                        bad.add(id, &format!("{name}.{column}={value}; {error}"));
                        detailed = true;
                    }
                }
                if !detailed {
                    bad.add(id, &error.to_string());
                }
            }
            for (column, kind) in &table.columns {
                if let Err(error) =
                    super::activation::validate_assets(tx, kind, row.field(column).ok())
                {
                    bad.add(id, &format!("{name}.{column}: {error}"));
                }
            }
            Ok(())
        })?;
        if bad.count != 0 {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Structural {
                code: "E_ADOPT",
                message: format!(
                    "table {name}: {}; rewrite: repair the named values under the current release, or deploy a bridge with optional/literal-default columns using --restore-dormant, backfill through a bridge mutation, then deploy required types; inspect: snapback4 sql \"SELECT * FROM [{name}]\" --release <dir>",
                    bad.describe()
                ),
            });
        }

    pub(super) fn activate(

        schema: &Schema,

        expected_release: Option<u64>,
        expected_backend: Option<&crate::activation::Backend>,
    ) -> Result<Committed<(u64, crate::store_port::OpeningMetadata)>, RuntimeError> {

            admission::check_state_predicates(schema, program)?;
        }

            for table in crate::ir::read_sites(program)
                .into_iter()
                .map(|s| s.table)
                .chain(crate::ir::write_tables(program).into_iter().map(|(t, _)| t))
            {
                schema.refuse_private(&table)?;
            }
        }
        for schedule in &schema.schedules {
            if !schedule.valid()
            {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!(
                    "schedule {} has an invalid period or offset",
                    schedule.name
                )));
            }
            let Some(program) = programs
                .get(&schedule.target)
                .filter(|p| matches!(p.kind, ProgramKind::Job | ProgramKind::Action))
            else {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!(
                    "schedule {} names missing job or action {}; restore or remove the schedule",
                    schedule.name, schedule.target
                )));
            };
            if program.kind == ProgramKind::Action {
                let seat = schedule
                    .service
                    .as_deref()
                    .filter(|seat| crate::identity::is_service(seat))
                    .ok_or_else(|| {
                        RuntimeError::Schema(
                            "a scheduled action requires its declared service seat".into(),
                        )
                    })?;
                actions::authorize(program, seat)?;
            } else if schedule.service.is_some() {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(
                    "a scheduled job cannot name a service seat".into(),
                ));
            }
            if program.args.iter().any(|(name, kind)| {
                !matches!(name.as_str(), "periodStart" | "periodEnd")
                    || *kind != crate::ir::ArgType::Time
            }) {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(
                    "scheduled work takes periodStart: time and periodEnd: time, or nothing".into(),
                ));
            }
        }

        let population=std::cell::Cell::new(None);
        Committer::run_committed_with(store, |store| {
        let outcome=store.activate_at_with_launch(schema, Some(programs), false, now, allow_feed_adoption, |release_check| {
            release_check.check_selected_release(expected_release)?;
            if let Some(backend) = expected_backend { release_check.check_backend(backend)?; }
            if let Some(options)=release {
                options.check(release_check)?;
                *prior_items.borrow_mut()=release_check.activation_inventory()?;
                release_check.enable_dormant()?;
                release_check.prepare_release_metadata(options.native_digest.as_deref().unwrap_or(""), options.restore_dormant,options.visibility.as_ref())?;
            }
            Ok(())
        }, |tx, changed, report| {
            let now=now.max(ActivationReader::time_audience_clock(tx)?);
            if let Some(options)=release {tx.set_active_release(options.release)?;}
            let repair_live_sums = tx.live_sum_marker()?.as_deref() != Some("1");

//! Complete operator inspection over one read-only retained snapshot.
// @ref LLP 3000.037.000#9 — retained cells supplement active images once per id.
use super::Options;
use snapback4_core::store_port::AnyInspection;
use serde_json::json;
use std::path::Path;
pub(super) fn run(path: &Path, table: &str, options: &Options) -> i32 {
    let inspected=(|| {
        let conn=AnyInspection::open(path)?;
        conn.begin_snapshot()?;
        conn.merged_records(table)
    })();
    match inspected {
        Ok(rows) => {
            let rows:Vec<_>=rows.iter().map(snapback4_core::wire::to_json).collect();
            if options.json { println!("{}",json!({"rows":rows,"more":false,"limit":null,"inspection":"active and retained records, merged by id"})); }
            else { for row in rows {println!("{row}");} }
            0
        }
        Err((code,message)) => { eprintln!("{code}: {message}"); 1 }
    }
}
//! Operator snapshots preserve tagged values even when historical column types differ.
// @ref LLP 3000.037.000#9 — merge current and retained images once per id, never via the active SQL scalar type.
use super::{Inspection, Result, store_error};
use crate::{Value, schema::{Schema, materialize_optional_fields},inspection::{InspectionCatalog,internal}};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
impl Inspection {
    pub fn merged_records(&self, name: &str) -> Result<Vec<Value>> {
        if internal(name) {return Err(("E_SQL_INTERNAL","data reads application tables only".into()));}
        let (_,encoded)=self.active_schema_record()?;
        let active:Schema=serde_json::from_str(&encoded).map_err(|e|("E_SCHEMA",e.to_string()))?;
        active.refuse_private(name).map_err(|e|("E_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_PRIVATE",e.to_string()))?;
        let schema=self.retained_schema(active.clone())?;
        schema.refuse_private(name).map_err(|e|("E_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_PRIVATE",e.to_string()))?;
        let definition=schema.table(name).ok_or_else(||("E_SCHEMA",format!("no table {name}")))?;
        if definition.ephemeral.is_some() {return Err(("E_SCHEMA",format!("data {name}: ephemeral rows live only in serving memory; no disk snapshot")));}
        let number:i64=self.conn.query_row("SELECT num FROM _catalog WHERE name=?1",[name],|row|row.get(0)).map_err(store_error)?;
        let mut merged=BTreeMap::<String,BTreeMap<String,Value>>::new();
        // Active images win, including explicit null; retained cells fill only absent names.
        let mut query=self.conn.prepare("SELECT id,data,0 AS rank FROM r WHERE t=?1 UNION ALL SELECT id,data,1 FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 UNION ALL SELECT id,data,2 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 ORDER BY rank,id").map_err(store_error)?;
        let rows=query.query_map([number],|row|Ok((row.get::<_,String>(0)?,row.get::<_,String>(1)?,row.get::<_,i64>(2)?))).map_err(store_error)?;
        for row in rows {
            let (id,data,rank)=row.map_err(store_error)?;
            let mut value:Value=serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e|("E_SCHEMA",e.to_string()))?;
            if rank==0 {if let Some(table)=active.table(name) {materialize_optional_fields(&mut value,table);}}
            let Value::Row(fields)=value else {return Err(("E_SCHEMA","inspection row has no row image".into()));};
            let target=merged.entry(id.clone()).or_default();
            target.entry("id".into()).or_insert(Value::Id(id));
            for (column,value) in fields.iter() {
                if definition.columns.contains_key(column) {target.entry(column.clone()).or_insert_with(||value.clone());}
            }
        }
        let mut query=self.conn.prepare("SELECT id,column_name,data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 ORDER BY id,column_name").map_err(store_error)?;
        let cells=query.query_map([number],|row|Ok((row.get::<_,String>(0)?,row.get::<_,String>(1)?,row.get::<_,String>(2)?))).map_err(store_error)?;
        for cell in cells {
            let (id,column,data)=cell.map_err(store_error)?;
            // Cell-only residue cannot create a row that no retained image owns.
            if let Some(target)=merged.get_mut(&id) {
                if definition.columns.contains_key(&column) {
                    let value:Value=serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e|("E_SCHEMA",e.to_string()))?;
                    target.entry(column).or_insert(value);
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(merged.into_values().map(|fields|Value::Row(fields.into())).collect())
    }
}

CLI/provision failures and the generated business hook preserves refusal codes,
never retries writes, and recommends fresh owners/groups or deliberate cleanup.
Changing limits or resetting shared production is not a proof repair.
The proof digest derives exact declarations and includes production account and
private roster shapes, so builders need not read proof.js.

Administrative data --all and snapshotAll use a read-only retained
inspection adapter in one snapshot. They merge active rows, retained rows and
retired cells once per ID, retaining explicit nulls and preferring active cells
when representations overlap. Tagged historical values are decoded in their own
stored representation, not projected through the current column type. Cell-only
residue cannot resurrect a missing row. Deleted rows remain deleted. Ordinary limited
data keeps its active-only view; ephemeral tables refuse disk snapshots.
This changes presentation, not release storage or application read authority.
Releases --json remains the separate retained-source history inspection.

//! A day-one headless client must keep its own handles and module boundary.
// @ref LLP 3000.037.000#9 — capture before editing; never overwrite historical evidence.
use std::{path::Path, io};
use serde_json::json;

pub(super) fn files(dir: &Path, sources: &[(String, String)]) -> io::Result<Vec<(&'static str, String)>> {
    if dir.join("v1").exists() { return Ok(Vec::new()); }
    let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(sources);
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() { return Err(io::Error::other("cannot freeze an unchecked v1 client; fix current schema first")); }
    let mut files = Vec::new();
    fn push(files: &mut Vec<(&'static str,String)>, path: String, text: String) {
        files.push((Box::leak(path.into_boxed_str()),text));
    }
    fn walk(root: &Path, relative: &Path, files: &mut Vec<(&'static str,String)>) -> io::Result<()> {
        let path = root.join(relative);
        if !path.exists() { return Ok(()); }
        if path.symlink_metadata()?.file_type().is_symlink() { return Err(io::Error::other(format!("frozen client refuses symlink {}", path.display()))); }
        if path.is_dir() {
            if matches!(path.file_name().and_then(|n|n.to_str()),Some("node_modules"|"dist"|"target"|".git")) { return Ok(()); }
            for entry in std::fs::read_dir(path)? { walk(root, &relative.join(entry?.file_name()),files)?; }
        } else if matches!(path.extension().and_then(|s|s.to_str()), Some("mjs"|"cjs"|"js"|"ts"|"tsx"|"jsx")) || path.file_name().is_some_and(|n|n=="package.json") {
            push(files,format!("v1/{}",relative.display()),std::fs::read_to_string(path)?);
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    // Save application modules, not node_modules, stores, credentials or build output.
    for path in ["scripts", "src", "app"] { walk(dir,Path::new(path),&mut files)?; }
    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(dir)? {
        let entry=entry?;
        if entry.path().is_file() && entry.path().extension().is_some_and(|e|matches!(e.to_str(),Some("mjs"|"js"|"ts"|"tsx"))) { walk(dir,Path::new(&entry.file_name()),&mut files)?; }
    }
    // Follow literal relative module imports beyond conventional source directories.
    // Runtime-computed paths remain application responsibilities, as in Node itself.
    let canonical_root=dir.canonicalize()?;
    let mut index=0;
    while index<files.len() {
        let (saved,text)=&files[index];
        let source=dir.join(saved.strip_prefix("v1/").unwrap());
        let mut imports=Vec::new();
        let bytes=text.as_bytes();let mut cursor=0;
        while cursor<bytes.len() {
            let quote=bytes[cursor];
            if !matches!(quote,b'\''|b'"') {cursor+=1;continue;}
            let start=cursor+1;let mut end=start;
            while end<bytes.len() && bytes[end]!=quote {if bytes[end]==b'\\' {end+=1;}end+=1;}
            if end>=bytes.len() {break;}
            let before=text[..cursor].trim_end();
            let module=before.ends_with("from")||before.ends_with("import")||before.ends_with("import(")||before.ends_with("require(");
            if module {if let Some(value)=text.get(start..end) {if value.starts_with("./")||value.starts_with("../") {imports.push(value.to_owned());}}}
            cursor=end+1;
        }
        for import in imports {
            let path=source.parent().unwrap().join(import);
            if !path.exists() {continue;}
            let canonical=path.canonicalize()?;
            let relative=canonical.strip_prefix(&canonical_root).map_err(|_|io::Error::other("frozen relative import escapes the app; keep client modules inside the project"))?;
            let saved=format!("v1/{}",relative.display());
            if files.iter().any(|(name,_)|*name==saved) {continue;}
            if path.symlink_metadata()?.file_type().is_symlink() {return Err(io::Error::other("frozen module imports must not use symlinks"));}
            if path.is_file() {push(&mut files,saved,std::fs::read_to_string(path)?);}
        }
        index+=1;
    }
    for (name,text) in sources { push(&mut files,format!("v1/snapback/{name}"),text.clone()); }
    push(&mut files,"v1/snapback/generated/api.ts".into(),snapback4_lang::emit::typescript(&bound));
    push(&mut files,"v1/snapback/generated/package.json".into(),"{\"type\":\"module\"}\n".into());
    let package: serde_json::Value = std::fs::read(dir.join("package.json")).ok().and_then(|b|serde_json::from_slice(&b).ok()).unwrap_or_default();
    let command=package["scripts"]["client:calls"].as_str();
    let mut frozen=json!({"private":true,"type":"module","scripts":{"client:calls":"node .snapback4-frozen-run.mjs"}});
    if package["scripts"].is_object() { frozen["scripts"]=package["scripts"].clone(); }
    frozen["scripts"]["client:calls"]=json!("node .snapback4-frozen-run.mjs");
    if let Some(command)=command { frozen["scripts"]["client:calls:frozen"]=json!(command); }
    for group in ["dependencies","devDependencies"] { if package[group].is_object() { frozen[group]=package[group].clone(); } }
    push(&mut files,"v1/package.json".into(),super::pretty(&frozen));
    push(&mut files,"v1/.snapback4-frozen-run.mjs".into(),include_str!("frozen-run.mjs").into());
    Ok(files)
}
// API signatures: snapback4 guide api proof | client | auth | generated
// Frozen source uses its saved API. Inline users are adapted for legacy file readers.
import { mkdtemp, writeFile, rm, readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join, dirname } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
const cwd = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const pkg = JSON.parse(await readFile(join(cwd, "package.json"), "utf8"));
if (!pkg.scripts?.["client:calls:frozen"]) throw Error("No day-one client:calls was present at capture; add a business client before claiming frozen-client coverage.");
let temporary;
const env = { ...process.env };
try {
  if (env.CALLS_USERS?.trimStart().startsWith("{")) {
    const users = JSON.parse(env.CALLS_USERS);
    if (!users || Array.isArray(users) || Object.values(users).some(batch => !Array.isArray(batch))) throw Error("CALLS_USERS must be a role-to-account-arrays object");
    temporary = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "snapback4-v1-users-"));
    env.CALLS_USERS = join(temporary, "users.json");
    await writeFile(env.CALLS_USERS, JSON.stringify(users), {mode:0o600});
  }
  const child = spawn("npm", ["run", "--silent", "client:calls:frozen"], {cwd,env,stdio:"inherit"});
  const signals = ["SIGTERM", "SIGINT"];
  const forward = signal => { try { child.kill(signal); } catch {} };
  const handlers = signals.map(signal => {const fn=()=>forward(signal);process.on(signal,fn);return fn;});
  try {
    const result = await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{child.once("error",reject);child.once("exit",(code,signal)=>resolve({code,signal}));});
    process.exitCode = result.code ?? 1;
  } finally { signals.forEach((signal,i)=>process.off(signal,handlers[i])); }
} finally { if (temporary) await rm(temporary,{recursive:true,force:true}); }
//! Wave-33 day-two reading and frozen-client module failures.
use std::{fs,process::Command};
const BIN:&str=env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_snapback4");
#[test]
fn wave33_029_024_011_023_033_frozen_client_has_own_esm_api_and_inline_users() {
    let dir=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("dx16-freeze-{}",std::process::id()));
    fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("snapback")).unwrap();fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("scripts")).unwrap();
    fs::write(dir.join("snapback/schema.q"),"use identity password\ntable notes:\n  title: text <=80\n  public 'notes'\n  online only\nquery list():\n  return notes first 20 by .id\n").unwrap();
    fs::write(dir.join("package.json"),r#"{"scripts":{"client:calls":"node scripts/client-calls.mjs"}}"#).unwrap();
    fs::write(dir.join("scripts/client-calls.mjs"),"import {readFileSync,statSync} from 'node:fs';\nimport {api} from '../snapback/generated/api.ts';\nconst users=JSON.parse(readFileSync(process.env.CALLS_USERS));\nif(users.member[0].email!=='inline@example.test'||!api.list)throw Error('missing users or frozen handle');\nconsole.log(process.env.CALLS_USERS);\nconsole.log(statSync(process.env.CALLS_USERS).mode&511);\n").unwrap();
    let run=|args:&[&str]|Command::new(BIN).current_dir(&dir).args(args).env("SNAPBACK4_INIT_INSTALL","0").output().unwrap();
    let preview=run(&["init","--adapt","--day2"]);assert!(preview.status.success(),"{}",String::from_utf8_lossy(&preview.stderr));assert!(!dir.join("v1").exists());assert!(!dir.join(".snapback4/guide-seen.json").exists());
    let result=run(&["init","--adapt","--day2","--apply"]);assert!(result.status.success(),"{}",String::from_utf8_lossy(&result.stderr));assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&result.stdout).starts_with("# Day two:"));
    let package:serde_json::Value=serde_json::from_slice(&fs::read(dir.join("v1/package.json")).unwrap()).unwrap();assert_eq!(package["type"],"module");
    let frozen=fs::read(dir.join("v1/snapback/generated/api.ts")).unwrap();
    fs::write(dir.join("snapback/schema.q"),"table other:\n  title: text <=80\n  public 'other'\n  online only\n").unwrap();
    assert!(run(&["init","--adapt","--day2","--apply"]).status.success());assert_eq!(fs::read(dir.join("v1/snapback/generated/api.ts")).unwrap(),frozen);
    let child=Command::new("npm").args(["--prefix","v1","run","--silent","client:calls"]).current_dir(&dir).env("CALLS_USERS",r#"{"member":[{"email":"inline@example.test"}]}"#).output().unwrap();
    assert!(child.status.success(),"{}{}",String::from_utf8_lossy(&child.stdout),String::from_utf8_lossy(&child.stderr));let output=String::from_utf8(child.stdout).unwrap();let path=output.lines().next().unwrap();assert!(!std::path::Path::new(path).exists(),"private normalized users cleaned");assert!(output.lines().any(|l|l=="384"),"mode 0600");
    fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
}
#[test]
fn wave33_001_022_011_021_full_lists_sections_and_repeats_delta() {
    let dir=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("dx16-guide-{}",std::process::id()));fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("snapback")).unwrap();
    let run=|args:&[&str]|String::from_utf8(Command::new(BIN).current_dir(&dir).args(args).output().unwrap().stdout).unwrap();
    for name in ["deploy","production","evolution"] {let text=run(&["guide",name,"--full"]);assert!(text.len()<=8000);assert!(!text.contains("// day-two-production.mjs"));}
    let first=run(&["guide","deploy","--section","Keep v1 clients working"]);assert!(first.contains("baseline"));let again=run(&["guide","deploy","--section","Keep v1 clients working"]);assert!(again.contains("new: 0 blocks"));assert!(again.len()<first.len());
    assert_eq!(first,run(&["guide","deploy","--section","Keep v1 clients working","--repeat"]));
    fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
}
#[test]
fn wave33_muse_021_all_merges_retired_cells_and_narrowed_rows_across_retained_releases() {
    let dir=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("dx16-retained-{}",std::process::id()));
    fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("snapback")).unwrap();
    let one="table notes:\n  title: text <=80\n  color: text <=20 ?\n  public 'notes'\n  online only\n";
    let two=one.replace("  color: text <=20 ?\n", "").replace("text <=80","text <=4");
    fs::write(dir.join("snapback/schema.q"),one).unwrap();
    let run=|args:&[&str]|{let result=Command::new(BIN).current_dir(&dir).args(args).output().unwrap();assert!(result.status.success(),"{}{}",String::from_utf8_lossy(&result.stdout),String::from_utf8_lossy(&result.stderr));result};
    run(&["deploy","production"]);
    let bound=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),one.into())]);
    let definition=bound.schema.table("notes").unwrap();
    let rows:Vec<_>=[serde_json::json!({"id":"fits","title":"tiny","color":"blue"}),serde_json::json!({"id":"wide","title":"historical wide value","color":"red"})].into_iter().map(|row|("notes".to_owned(),snapback4_core::wire::row_from_json(definition,&row).unwrap())).collect();
    let mut engine=snapback4_core::Engine::open(snapback4_core::Store::open(&dir.join("production/store.sqlite")).unwrap()).unwrap();engine.seed(rows).unwrap();drop(engine);
    fs::write(dir.join("snapback/schema.q"),two).unwrap();run(&["deploy","production"]);
    let inspect=|| {let out=run(&["data","notes","--all","--release","production","--json"]);serde_json::from_slice::<serde_json::Value>(&out.stdout).unwrap()};
    let all=inspect();let rows=all["rows"].as_array().unwrap();assert_eq!(rows.len(),2);assert_eq!(rows[0]["title"],"tiny");assert_eq!(rows[0]["color"],"blue");assert_eq!(rows[1]["title"],"historical wide value");assert_eq!(rows[1]["color"],"red");
    run(&["rollback","production","--to","1"]);assert_eq!(inspect()["rows"],all["rows"]);
    run(&["rollback","production","--to","latest"]);assert_eq!(inspect()["rows"],all["rows"]);
    fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
}

import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
/** A unique row name or intent ID for this run; does not bypass per-owner caps. */
export function uniqueName(prefix = "proof"): string {
  if (!/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,31}$/.test(prefix)) throw Error("E_PROOF_USAGE: uniqueName prefix must be 1..32 ASCII name characters, starting with a letter");
  return `${prefix}-${randomUUID()}`;
}

codex
**MATERIAL — not READY.**

1. **Frozen capture duplicates an existing generated API.** In [frozen.rs:60](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b33-tok1/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/verbs/init/frozen.rs:60), import traversal captures `snapback/generated/api.ts` when an existing client imports it. [Line 65](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b33-tok1/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/verbs/init/frozen.rs:65) then unconditionally adds the same destination again. Applying uses `create_new`, so an already-checked day-one app fails with `EEXIST`, leaving a partial `v1/` that subsequent captures skip. **Fix:** reserve generated destinations or deduplicate the plan, keeping the checked generated API authoritative. Add a regression with an existing imported API before adaptation.

2. **Guide search errors escape the byte budget.** [main.rs:193](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b33-tok1/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/main.rs:193) prints `Err(problem)` without bounding it. The inexpensive probe `guide deploy --find 'release NO_SUCH_WORD'` emitted **21,784 bytes**: fallback matches travel through this error branch. **Fix:** apply the output budget to errors as well as successful selections, preserving the refusal status and narrowing advice. Add a regression asserting bounded failed searches.

**Bounded minor observations**

- [pages.rs:12](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b33-tok1/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/guide/pages.rs:12) relocates original introductory content beneath `## Opening`, while `--section opening` still selects the preceding wrapper. For large cards such as deploy, that command returns navigation prose instead of the original opening. Align named opening selection with the flattened section.
- [frozen-run.mjs:14](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b33-tok1/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/verbs/init/frozen-run.mjs:14) normalizes inline users but leaves relative file paths unchanged despite execution under `v1/`. Document the required path base or resolve paths against the original invocation directory.

Read-only review; no builds or test suites run. Guide probes used the existing binary; the latest source was inspected separately.
